We can treat as polynomial approximation: We want B(x)=A(x)^2 to have coefficients as equal as possible over x^0..x^{2n-2}. This is like finding A with nonnegative coefficients approximating a flat denominator. If B(x) ≈ (1/(2n-1)) (1 - x^{2n-1})/(1-x) = uniform polynomial, then A(x) ≈ (1/√(2n-1)) sqrt((1 - x^{2n-1})/(1-x))^{1/2}. Square root of Dirichlet kernel? The coefficients of sqrt of uniform polynomial might be nonnegative? Let's test: A(x) = (1-x^n)/(1-x)?? square gives triangular. The sqrt of uniform? Let's think of local optimization algorithms for min max quadratic over simplex.

1. Projected gradient with acceleration (FISTA). Objective f(a)=max c_k. Use subgradient. On large n, can converge. Mirror descent may be slower.
2. Use "smoothing" and L-BFGS-B. We can optimize over a simplex with projected gradient and line search, but non-smooth.
3. Use "active set quadratic programming": At current a, active peaks K (top maybe 10). Approximate f(a+δ) ≈ max_{k∈K} [c_k + 2∑ a_i a_{k-i} δ_i + δ_i δ_{k-i}]. Could solve a convex QP? The max of affine/quadratic? If we drop δδ term, it's linear constraints in δ: for active k, 2∑ a_i a_{k-i} δ_i ≤ M - c_k (negative) plus sum δ=0, a+δ≥0. This is LP for step. Use scipy linprog to find a feasible descent step. This is cutting-plane. Could be effective. We can solve small LP for δ (z a_i-1) with constraints for top peaks, bounds. Then update. More robust than subgradient.

LP step formulation:
Variables δ_j (length n). Constraints:
- sum δ_j = 0 (maintain sum)
- For each active peak k: 2∑_i a_i a_{k-i} δ_i ≤ c_k - M? Wait we want new max ≤ M_new < M. If we target reduce M by ε, require for all k: c_k + 2∑ a_i a_{k-i} δ_i ≤ M - ε (since ignore δ^2; plus ensure by small step maybe). For inactive with large c_k near M, include too. Use top K (all > M - tolerance). Also maintain a_j+δ_j ≥0 -> δ_j ≥ -a_j.

Update strategy:
- Given a, M, solve LP get g (raw). Normalize b=g/g.sum(). Evaluate score_b.
- Consider mix f = (1-λ)a + λ b for λ in [0,1]. Because f sum=1. Compute score for grid λ 100 points (fast conv each maybe 0.1ms*100=10ms for n=1000; OK). Choose best. If better, set a=best mix. Repeat until no improvement or time.
- If LP in any iteration improves, continue; else stop.

Coordinate descent:
For each coordinate j, adjust a_j optimally keeping others scaled to sum 1. The objective as function of v = a_j (with others scaled by (1-v)/(1-old)) is max over k of quadratic functions in v: each c_k(v) is a quadratic (because a_i v terms). If both factors include j, term v^2 (no scaling? other is j itself, not scaled). Else term v * scaled_other. All A,B could be negative? Scaling others decreases with v (s positive), so coefficient for v*s(v) is linear * (1-v) => quadratic with negative A? Let's derive: a_j' = v, a_i'=a_i*(1-v)/(1-old). For term with one j and one i≠j: v * a_i s(v) = v a_i (1-v)/(1-old) = [a_i/(1-old)](v - v^2), so A negative! For term with j both: v^2. rows=[], cols=[], data=[].
For k in 0..2n-2:
  js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)
  for j in js..je: rows.append(k); cols.append(j); data.append(a[k-j])
Total nonzeros ~n(2n-1)-tri? ~n(2n-1) - n(n-1) = n^2? Specifically number pairs i,j = n^2. Because convolution matrix rows constraints correspond all pairs i,j: row = i+j, col=j (or variable g_j). ### Smoothing + L-BFGS
Use `scipy.optimize.minimize` with method='L-BFGS-B' on variables a (positive) with simplex projection? L-BFGS-B can't handle simplex equality easily. We can optimize log z unconstrained: a=softmax(z). Objective = score(softmax(z)). It's smooth (max nonsmooth but can smooth with logsumexp). Gradient = chain rule. This is essentially mirror descent but with L-BFGS approximate Hessian, might be faster. However dimension n ~768, L-BFGS-B many function evals; each FFT. Could be okay for small n. But nonconvex, may get stuck. Use as polish from good a.

Smooth objective: Φ_T(z) = (1/T) log ∑ exp(T c_k) plus maybe constant. For large T approx max. Gradient w.r.t z_j: ∑_k w_k (∂c_k/∂z_j). ∂c_k/∂z_j =? a_j times (∂c/∂a_j): 2 a_j * h_j (as subgradient) but sum a_j? Derivative of c_k =∑ a_i a_{k-i} wrt a_j = 2∑ a_l a_{k-j-l}. Then wrt z_j = a_j * that. We can compute via subgradient times a. L-BFGS-B with logistic softmax. But n 768, gradient O(Kn). OK.

### Implementing `compute_subgradient_top(a, peak_indices, weights)` efficiently
Previous loops. To speed, for each k, we need add `2*w_k * a[k - (js:je+1)]`. Let's verify orientation. c_k = ∑_{j=js}^{je} a_j a_{k-j}. Derivative wrt a_j: 2 a_{k-j} (unless j=k-j but convolution treats terms? For k=2j, term a_j*a_j appears once in sum? In convolution c_k = ∑_{i} a_i a_{k-i}; if i=k/2, term a_i^2 appears once, derivative is 2a_i. The formula 2 a_{k-j} works. Good.) So for j slice, add 2*w_k*a[k-j]. In numpy: `idx = np.arange(js, je+1); g[js:je+1] += 2*w_k*a[k - idx]`. Since k-idx ranges k-je..k-js, within 0..n-1. Could vectorize by preindexed arrays but OK.

### Fast convolve with real FFT
```
def fast_convolve(a):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; size=1 << (L-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a, size); return np.fft.irfft(A*A, size)[:L]
```
Ensure no tiny negative due to numeric; for max use max(c, maybe negative). Could clip c = np.maximum(c,0) for score? Convolution of nonnegative should nonnegative; FFT errors may cause -1e-18. Use max of raw is fine.

### Evaluate fast score on normalized a
```
def fast_score(a):
    conv=fast_convolve(a); return 2*len(a)*conv.max()
```
For unnormalized, score=2*n*maxconv/sum^2; but keep normalized.

```
a = normalize(a); n=len(a); best_obj=fast_score(a); best_a=a; stall=0; it=0; K=min(48,n);
while time remaining > 5:
  conv=fast_convolve(a); M=conv.max()
  # choose active top K where value > M - 0.1*M? but top K enough
  if len(conv)>K: idx=np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
  else idx=np.arange(len(conv))
  # ensure idx sorted? not necessary
  # Build sparse A_ub for constraints h·δ + τ ≤ M - conv[idx]
  # variables x = [δ (n), τ]
  rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]; bvec=[]
  for t,k in enumerate(idx):
    js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k);
    if je<js: continue
    j=np.arange(js,je+1)
    rows.extend([t]*len(j)); cols.extend(j.tolist()); data.extend((2*a[k-j]).tolist())
    bvec.append(M - conv[k])
  A = csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(len(bvec), n+1))
  # add tau column ones: use A[:, n] = 1? With csr, add separate arrays.
  # Better include in coo: cols tau = n, data=1 for each row.
  cvec array n+1 zeros, cvec[n]=-1
  bounds list: for each j (a_j, U) ; tau (0, M*0.5)
  res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, A_eq=[ones n,0], b_eq=[0], bounds=bounds, method='highs')
  if res.status==0:
    delta=res.x[:n]; tau=res.x[n]
  else: maybe no step
  # line search
  best_alpha=0; best_trial=best_obj (or current? use current M*2n)
  for alpha in [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0, maybe 0.2...]
      trial=a+alpha*delta; if min<tr- small continue? normalize (sum should 1 if sumdelta=0; numerical normalize)
      obj=fast_score(trial)
      if obj<best_trial: best...
  if best_alpha>0 and best_trial < best_obj*(1-1e-8): accept; best_*=; stall=0
  else:
     # try smaller random step? or reduce K? if no K >=?

     stall +=1

        rng noise small; a=normalize(best_a+noise*0.001); best_obj=fast_score; stall=0
     else break? 
```

### Smooth mirror descent implementation
```
def smooth_mirror_descent(a0, time_limit, T_init=1000, T_max=5000, lr_init=0.01):
  z=np.log(np.maximum(a0,1e-300)); z-=z.max()
  a=softmax(z); best=score
  T=T_init; lr=lr_init; stall=0
  while time:
    conv=fast_convolve(a)
    cmax=conv.max()
    # weights softmax with T but scale stable
    scaled=(conv-cmax)*T; scaled -= scaled.max(); w=exp(scaled); w/=sum
    # gradient dense via FFT
    g=weighted_gradient_fft(a,w)
    # backtracking
    best_alpha... try lr*0.5^i:
       z_trial=z-lr_trial*g; z_trial-=z_trial.max(); a_trial=softmax; obj=fast_score
    accept best; update z; a=softmax
    if new obj < best: best...
    T*=1.01? maybe increase to sharpen; lr decay; restart noise if stall
```
Use dense gradient via FFT. Weight `w` length L=2n-1. `weighted_gradient_fft`:
```
def weighted_gradient_fft(a, w):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; size=1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size); W=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],size); conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,size)[:3*n-2]
    return 2.0 * conv[L-1 - np.arange(n)]
```
Check indices: For n=1, L=1, size? works. For n length, conv index m=L-1-j. We need ensure conv array length at least m+n? m ranges 0 to L-1. size ok.
Test with a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. g_0=2(w0 a0 + w1 a1)? c0 derivative=2 a0 a0=2w0? Wait c0=a0^2, derivative wrt a0=2a0. F_a0=2w0 a0 + for c1? c1=2a0a1, derivative=2w1a1. So F_a0=2a0 w0+2a1 w1. g_0 formula conv_rev[L-1]=∑ a_i rev_w[L-1-i]. rev_w indices: [w2,w1,w0]. L-1=1. conv_rev[1]=a0*rev_w[1]+a1*rev_w[0]=a0 w1 + a1 w2? That's not intended. Let's re-derive.
We want g_j = ∑_k w_k a_{k-j} for k-j ∈[0,n-1]. For each j, sum over l=k-j: ∑_l a_l w_{l+j}. So g_j = ∑_{l=0}^{n-1} a_l w_{l+j}. This is cross-correlation with positive lag.
Let rev_w = w[::-1], length L, index r=q: rev_w[q]=w[L-1-q]. Linear conv h = a * rev_w: h[m]=∑_l a_l rev_w[m-l] valid. Set m-l = L-1 - (l+j) ? Want rev_w[m-l]=w_{l+j} => L-1-(m-l)=l+j => L-1-m+l = l+j => m=L-1-j. Yes h[L-1-j]=∑_l a_l w_{l+j}. Good. For n=2,w length3. g_0: l=0 w0 + l=1 w1. h[L-1-j]=h[1]. h[1]=a0*rev_w[1]+a1*rev_w[0]=a0*w1 + a1*w2. That gave w1,w2. Why? Because m-l for l=1: m=1,l=1 =>0 rev_w0=w2. But w_{l+j}=w_1 for l=1? Let's plug formula: L=3,j=0, l=1 => l+j=1. Need rev_w index = L-1 - (l+j)=3-1-1=1, not m-l? m=L-1-j=2? Ah m=L-1-j =2, not 1. I made off by 1: L-1-j for L=3,j=0 =>2. h[2]=a0*rev_w[2]+a1*rev_w[1]=a0*w0+a1*w1. Good. So index m=L-1-j? But L-1=2, j=0 =>2. General m=L-1-j? Wait L-1-j for j=0 is L-1=2, yes. We earlier said L-1-j. Good. For j=1,m=1: h[1]=a0*w1+a1*w2 correct. So g_j = h[L-1 - j]. Since L=2n-1, L-1=2n-2. h length n+L-1=3n-2, max index 3n-3. m range 0 to2n-2, ok. So `idx = (2*n-2) - np.arange(n)`. Good.
Thus `g=2*h[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]`.

### L-BFGS smooth implementation
Use `objective_grad(z, T)` returns F=smooth_max maybe, grad_z=a*(g-A). Since fast_score scale 2n constant, can ignore. For L-BFGS, scale doesn't matter much but gradient scale. Use F=smooth_max. Implement:
```
def smooth_obj_grad(z, T):
   a=softmax(z)
   c=fast_convolve(a); cmax=c.max()
   scaled=(c-cmax)*T; scaled-=scaled.max(); exps=np.exp(scaled); s=exps.sum()
   if s==0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1 else w=exps/s
   g=weighted_gradient_fft(a,w)
   A=np.dot(a,g)
   grad=z? a*(g-A)
   F=cmax + np.log(s)/T
   return F, grad
```
Potential overflow if T*(c-cmax) large negative no; subtract max. If T large, exps zeros except peak; fine.

### Implement `top_mirror_descent` robust
```
def top_mirror_descent(a0, time_limit, top_k=32, temp_init=None, lr_init=0.1, verbose=False):
  a=normalize
  z=np.log(a); z-=z.max()
  temp = temp_init or 1.0/n? Since c scale; use 1/(5*n)? Previous temp_init 0.0005 for n=768 ~1/(1536). Use temp=1/(5*n) maybe 0.00026. 
  lr=lr_init; stall=0; best=score
  it=0
  while time:
    conv=fast_convolve(a); M=conv.max()
    K=min(top_k, len(conv)); idx=argpartition...
    pv=conv[idx]; scaled=(pv-pv.max())/temp; scaled-=max; w=exp; w/=sum
    g=compute_subgradient_top(a,idx,w)
    gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
    if gnorm>1e-15: g/=gnorm
    # backtracking
    best_alpha; for trial in [lr,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]
       z_t=z-trial*g; z_t-=max; a_t=softmax; obj=fast_score
    accept; if improved best; temp*=1.001; lr decay; restart if stall
  return best
```

If all peaks equal, temp small -> uniform over K.

Start and increase to 0.5M? Actually smaller temp focuses top; too small may overfocus single peak. Use temp = 0.05*M initially, grow to 0.3M.
`temp = 0.05 * max(M,1e-12); temp = min(temp*1.002, 0.3*M)`.

### `optimize_candidate` design
```
def optimize_candidate(a, time_limit, verbose=False):
    a=normalize(a)
    best_a=a; best_obj=fast_score(a)
    n=len(a); start_time=time.monotonic(); end=start_time+time_limit
    no_improve=0
    methods_cycle=0
    while time.monotonic() < end - 5:
       t_lp = min(40, (end-now)*0.4)
       a2,obj2=lp_active_descent(best_a, t_lp, verbose)
       if obj2 < best_obj - 1e-9: accept; no=0
       else: no+=1
       t_md = min(40, (end-now)*0.3)
       a2,obj2=top_mirror_descent(a2 if improved else best_a, t_md, verbose)
       ...
       # occasional L-BFGS
       if no_improve>=2 and remaining>30:
          a2,obj2=smooth_lgbfgs(best_a, min(30, rem*0.2))
          ...
       if no_improve>=5:
          # perturb
          noise=rng? use global rng? We can create local rng with time? top-level function can use np.random.default_rng(int(time*1000))
          perturb...
          no_improve=0
    # final polish with remaining
    if remaining>20:
       a2,obj2=top_mirror_descent(best_a, remaining-10, use dense? )
    return best_a,best_obj
```

### `top_mirror_descent` details with direct subgradient
Use `temp` and top_k. For very large n, top_k=64.
```
def top_mirror_descent(a0, time_limit, top_k=32, lr_init=0.2, seed=0, verbose=False):
   a = normalize(a0); z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.max()
   a=softmax(z); best=fast_score(a); best_a=a
   n=len(a); t0=time.monotonic(); lr=lr_init; it=0; stale=0
   temp_factor=0.05
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
      conv=fast_convolve(a); M=conv.max()
      K=min(top_k, len(conv))
      idx=np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:] if K<len(conv) else np.arange(len(conv))
      pv=conv[idx]; pvmin=pv.min(); gap=max(M-pvmin, 1e-15)
      temp=max(1e-12, temp_factor*gap)
      scaled=(pv - M)/temp # <=0; no max? 
      # Better scaled=(pv - pv.max())/temp
      expv=np.exp(scaled - scaled.max()); w=expv/expv.sum()
      g=compute_subgradient_top(a, idx, w)
      gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gnorm>1e-14: g/=gnorm
      # line search
      best_obj_current=M; best_trial=M; best_alpha=0
      for alpha in [lr, 0.5*lr,0.25*lr,0.125*lr,0.0625*lr]:
         if alpha<=1e-10: continue
         zt=z-alpha*g; zt=zt-zt.max(); at=softmax(zt); obj=fast_score(at)
         if obj < best_trial - 1e-14:
             best_trial=obj; best_alpha=alpha
             if best_trial < M*0.999: break
      if best_alpha>0 and best_trial < M - 1e-14:
         z = z - best_alpha*g ; z-=z.max(); a=softmax(z); obj=fast_score(a)
         if obj<best-1e-12: best=obj; best_a=a; stale=0
         lr=min(lr*1.05, 1.0) # increase if improved?
      else:
         stale +=1
         lr=max(lr*0.5, 1e-4)
         if stale > 200:
            # random restart from best with noise
            rng = np.random.default_rng(it*1000003+7)
            a = normalize(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n)*0.002)
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.max()
            lr=lr_init*0.5; stale=0
      temp_factor = min(temp_factor*1.003, 0.5)
      lr = max(lr*0.9999, 1e-5)
      it+=1
      if verbose and it%100==0: print
   return best_a,best
```
Should we update a even if no improvement? No, keep best for z? We could keep current a to explore, but if not improving maybe wander. We use z update only if trial improved, else maybe no update. But then stuck. Instead apply best_alpha among trials even if not best? If best_trial not < current, all worse; don't update. Then only perturb. This is okay but may be slow.
Maybe apply a small step in direction with alpha=lr/10 even if not improve to escape? But objective nonconvex; need descent. Use perturb.
Set lr_init maybe 0.1; since g normalized, lr 0.1 changes z by 0.1 if gnorm normalized; too large? Backtracking will reduce. Good.

### `smooth_lgbfgs`
```
def smooth_lgbfgs(a0, time_limit, T_init=None, maxiter=100, seed=0):
  a=norm; best=fast_score(a); best_a=a
  z=np.log(a); z-=z.mean()
  T = T_init or max(500, 2*n)
  now=time; it=0
  while time < time_limit:
     obj = SmoothObjective(T)
     res=optimize.minimize(obj, z, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, maxiter=maxiter, options={'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':50, 'maxcor':10})
     if res.fun finite:
        a=softmax(res.x)
        sc=fast_score(a)
        if sc<best: update
        z=res.x; z-=mean
     # increase T or reduce
     T = min(T*2, 20*n)
     if no improve for 3: break
  return best
```
Potential minimizer may take long; maxiter small (50). For n=768, 2 runs maybe 5s. Good.

Need class uses top-level functions `softmax`, `fast_convolve`, `weighted_gradient_fft`.

### `softmax` top-level:
```
def softmax(z):
  z = np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
  zm = z - z.max()
  e=np.exp(zm)
  s=e.sum()
  if not np.isfinite(s) or s==0:
     # uniform over max
     z=z-z.max(); e=np.ones_like(z); ...? 
  return e/s
```
For z range huge, exp of negative large zero. If all zero (z all -inf?), shouldn't. Use `np.nan_to_num`.

```
def score_candidates(cands, limit=12):
  arr=[]
  for a in cands:
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: continue
    a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: continue
    a=a/s
    sc=2*a.size*fast_convolve(a).max()
    if np.isfinite(sc): arr.append((float(sc), a))
  if not arr: return []
  order=np.argsort([s for s,a in arr])
  # remove duplicates? None.
  return [arr[i][1] for i in order[:limit]]
```

```
def optimize_candidate(a, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=123):
    a=normalize(a)
    if a.size ==0: return a, inf
    best_a=a; best_obj=fast_score(a)
    n=len(a); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed*1000003 + n)
    t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+max(1.0,float(time_limit))
    no_improve=0
    method_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < end - 2:
        # LP if n<=3000 and remaining>5
        if n <= 3000:
            t_lp = min(35.0, (end - time.monotonic()) * 0.45)
            if t_lp > 5:
                a2, obj2 = lp_active_descent(best_a, t_lp, verbose=verbose)
                if obj2 < best_obj - 1e-9:
                    best_a=a2; best_obj=obj2; no_improve=0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
        # top MD
        t_md = min(35.0, (end - time.monotonic()) * 0.35)
        if t_md > 5:
            a2,obj2 = top_mirror_descent(best_a if no_improve==0 else a2? Use best_a)
            ...
        # dense smooth MD every 3 cycles
        if method_count % 3 == 2 and n<=4096:
            t_ds = min(25, rem*0.2); a2,obj2=dense_smooth_mirror...
        # L-BFGS if no_improve>=2
        if no_improve >= 2 and n<=1536 and rem>20:
            a2,obj2=smooth_lgbfgs(best_a, min(20, rem*0.2))
        # perturbation if no_improve >= 4
        if no_improve >= 4:
             noise_scale = 0.001 + 0.001*min(no_improve-4,5)
             a_p = normalize(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n)*noise_scale)
             # maybe also try shifted? 
             sc=fast_score(a_p)
             if sc < best_obj:
                best_a=a_p; best_obj=sc; no_improve=0
             else:
                # run short MD from perturbation with fixed time 15
                t_p=min(15, rem*0.1)
                a2,obj2=top_mirror_descent(a_p, t_p)
                if obj2<best_obj...
                no_improve=0 # allow more
        method_count +=1
    # final polish
    if time.monotonic() < end - 5:
       t_final = end - time.monotonic() - 2
       # choose smaller of top MD and dense
       a2,obj2=top_mirror_descent(best_a, t_final*0.6, top_k=64)
       if obj2<best_obj...
       # L-BFGS if time
       if time.monotonic() < end - 5:
           a2,obj2=smooth_lgbfgs(best_a, end-time-5)
    return best_a, best_obj
```

### `smooth_lgbfgs` might improve if T low. Use T series. Need ensure class no closures. Implementation:
```
class SmoothObjective:
    def __init__(self, T, n):
       self.T=float(T); self.n=int(n)
    def __call__(self, z):
       z=np.asarray(z,float)
       a=softmax(z); c=fast_convolve(a); mc=c.max()
       scaled=(c-mc)*self.T
       scaled = scaled - np.max(scaled)
       e=np.exp(scaled); s=e.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
           w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[np.argmax(c)]=1.0
       else: w=e/s
       g=weighted_gradient_fft(a,w)
       A=np.dot(a,g)
       grad=2.0*self.n*a*(g-A)
       F=2.0*self.n*(mc + np.log(s)/self.T) if s>0 else 2.0*self.n*mc
       if not np.isfinite(F): F=1e100
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=np.zeros_like(z)
       return F, grad
```
If T huge, log(s) okay. If s=0 impossible because max exp=1.

```
def smooth_lgbfgs(a0, time_limit, T_init=None, maxiter=80, seed=0):
  a=normalize; best...; n=...
  T=T_init or max(500, 2*n)
  z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
  t0=...; T_list = []
  no=0
  while time < limit:
     obj = SmoothObjective(T,n)
     # maybe set options based on n
     res=opt.minimize(obj,z,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,maxiter=maxiter,options={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxcor':10,'maxls':50})
     if res.fun < 1e90:
       a_new=softmax(res.x); sc=fast_score(a_new)
       if sc<best-1e-9: best...; z=res.x; z-=mean; no=0
       else: no+=1
     if no>=2: break
     T=min(T*1.5,20*n)
     if T>=20*n and no>=1: break
  return best
```

### `weighted_gradient_fft` size calculation
For n=1, L=1, size needed n+L-1=1, bit_length? `1 << (size_need - 1).bit_length()` works if size_need=1 ->0.bit_length=0 ->1. Good.
```
def fast_convolve(a):
 n=len(a); need=2*n-1; size=1 << (need-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a,size); return np.fft.irfft(A*A,size)[:need]
```

```
need=3*n-2; size=1 << (need-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a,size); W=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],size); h=np.fft.irfft(A*W,size)[:need]; return 2*h[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]
```

#### `score_candidates`
```
def score_candidates(cands, top=12):
    arr=[]
    for a in cands:
       a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
       if a.ndim!=1 or a.size==0: continue
       a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
       s=a.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s<1e-12: continue
       a=a/s
       conv=fast_convolve(a)
       sc=2.0*a.size*float(conv.max())
       if np.isfinite(sc): arr.append((sc,a))
    if not arr: return []
    vals=np.array([s for s,_ in arr], dtype=float)
    order=np.argsort(vals)
    res=[]
    for idx in order:
      if len(res)>=top: break
      res.append(arr[idx][1])
    return res
```
Could duplicate identical arrays; not problem.

#### `generate_initial_candidates`
Need avoid enormous time. Use sizes based on parent.
```
def generate_initial_candidates(parent, seed=0):
    cands=[]
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if parent is not None:
       p=normalize(parent)
       cands.append(p)
       nset=set([len(p)])
       for n in [256,512,768,900,1024,1152,1536,2048]:
          if n not in nset and 64<=n<=4096:
             cands.append(interpolate_sequence(p,n)); nset.add(n)
       sizes=[]
       for n in [max(256,len(p)//2), len(p), min(4096, 2*len(p))]:
          if n not in sizes: sizes.append(n)
    else:
       sizes=[512,768,1024]
    # For each size (limit 4)
    for n in sizes[:4]:
       i=np.arange(n)
       x=(i+0.5)/n
       xleft=(i+1.0)/(n-1+1e-9)? use (i+1)/n
       # simple powers
       for beta in [0.0,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5]:
          if beta==0: cands.append(np.ones(n))
          else: cands.append(np.power(i+1.0, -beta))
       # powers with right taper
       for beta in [0.0,0.25,0.5,0.8]:
          base = np.power(i+1.0, -beta) if beta>0 else np.ones(n)
          for gamma in [0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]:
              val=base * np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - i/(n-1), 1e-6), gamma)
              cands.append(val)
       # beta densities
       for ae in [-0.4, -0.2, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0]:
          for be in [-0.4, -0.2, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0]:
              xx = np.maximum(x, 1e-6)
              yy = np.maximum(1.0-x, 1e-6)
              val = np.exp(ae*np.log(xx)+be*np.log(yy))
              cands.append(val)
       # exponentials
       for lam in np.linspace(-3.0,3.0,13):
          cands.append(np.exp(lam*x))
       for lam in [1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]:
          for mu in [-2.0, 2.0, 6.0]:
              val=np.exp(lam*x + mu*(x-0.5)**2)
              cands.append(val)
       # two piece: power + gaussian
       for lb in [0.3,0.5,0.8]:
          base=np.power(i+1.0, -lb)
          for c in [0.3,0.5,0.7]:
             for s in [0.1,0.2,0.3]:
                val=base + 0.5*np.exp(-0.5*((x-c)/s)**2)
                cands.append(val)
    # random Fourier for base sizes (maybe 2)
    for n in sizes[:2]:
       x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
       for _ in range(30):
          z=np.zeros(n)
          D=int(rng.integers(2,10))
          for m in range(1,D+1):
             freq=float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))*m
             amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0))/(m**0.7)
             phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
             z += amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*freq*x + phase)
          cands.append(np.exp(z))
          # with left singular bias
          cands.append(np.exp(z)*np.power(np.arange(n)+1.0, -float(rng.uniform(0.0,0.7))))
    return cands
```
This might generate ~ for each size: powers 7 + taper 4*4=16 + beta 36 + exp13+12+ piece 3*3*3=27 = ~111 per size. For 3 sizes=333 + parent interp 8 + random 60*2=120 =>460. OK.
But beta with both negative produces large values at endpoints; normalize finite. Some may produce score high. Fine.
Could be memory ~460*1024*8=3.8MB.
Time scoring maybe 460 convs, okay.

We can reduce `sizes[:3]` if parent. Good.

#### `interpolate_sequence`
```
def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a=normalize(a)
    old_n=len(a)
    if old_n == new_n: return a.copy()
    old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    b=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return normalize(b)
```

#### `top_mirror_descent` detailed
Need avoid `np.argpartition` if K==len(conv). If K<=0? top_k at least1.
```
def top_mirror_descent(a0, time_limit, top_k=32, lr_init=0.2, seed=123, verbose=False):
    a=normalize(a0)
    if a.size==0: return a, float('inf')
    n=len(a)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=z-z.max()
    a=softmax(z)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=fast_score(a)
    t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+float(time_limit)
    lr=lr_init; stale=0; temp_factor=0.05; it=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed*1000003 + n)
    while time.monotonic() < end - 0.5:
       conv=fast_convolve(a); M=float(conv.max())
       if M<=0: break
       K=min(max(1,int(top_k)), conv.size)
       if K < conv.size:
          idx=np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
       else: idx=np.arange(conv.size)
       pv=conv[idx]
       pvmax=float(pv.max()); pvmin=float(pv.min())
       gap=max(pvmax-pvmin, pvmax*1e-6, 1e-15)
       temp=max(1e-15, temp_factor*gap)
       scaled=(pv - pvmax)/temp
       ex=np.exp(scaled); s=ex.sum()
       w=ex/s if s>0 else np.full(idx.size,1/idx.size)
       g=compute_subgradient_top(a, idx, w)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gnorm>1e-14: g=g/gnorm
       else: break
       best_trial=M; best_alpha=0.0
       # line search alphas adapt
       base=lr
       for mult in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
          alpha=base*mult
          if alpha<=1e-10: break
          zt=z-alpha*g; zt=zt-zt.max(); at=softmax(zt); obj=fast_score(at)
          if obj < best_trial - 1e-15:
             best_trial=obj; best_alpha=alpha
             if best_trial < M*0.999:
                break
       if best_alpha > 0 and best_trial < M - 1e-14:
          z = z - best_alpha*g; z=z-z.max(); a=softmax(z)
          obj=fast_score(a)
          if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
             best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); stale=0
             lr=min(lr*1.15, 1.0)
          else:
             stale +=1
       else:
          stale +=1
          lr=max(lr*0.5, 1e-5)
          if stale > 300:
             # restart from best with perturbation
             scale=0.001 + 0.001*min(stale/1000.0,1.0)
             ap=normalize(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n)*scale)
             a=ap; z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=z-z.max()
             lr=max(lr_init*0.5,0.05); stale=0
             # maybe set temp_factor 0.2
       temp_factor=min(temp_factor*1.004,0.5)
       lr=max(lr*0.99999, 1e-6)
       it+=1
       if verbose and it%200==0:
          print(f"    MD it {it} best {best_obj:.8f} lr {lr:.5f} tempf {temp_factor:.3f}")
    return best_a,best_obj
```
Potential: If we restart, `best_a` maybe better than `a`; z from perturbed best. Good.

#### `lp_active_descent` code detail

```
def lp_active_descent(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=0):
   a=normalize(a0); n=len(a); t0=... end=...
   best_a=a; best_obj=fast_score(a)
   K=min(48, n)
   stall=0; it=0
   rng=...
   while time.monotonic() < end - 2:
      conv=fast_convolve(a); M=float(conv.max()); L=conv.size
      if M<=0: break
      Kcurrent=min(K, max(8, L//4?))
      if Kcurrent < L:
         idx=np.argpartition(conv, -Kcurrent)[-Kcurrent:]
      else: idx=np.arange(L)
      # sort idx? not needed
      rows_list=[]; cols_list=[]; data_list=[]; bvec=[]
      for t,k in enumerate(idx):
         k=int(k); js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)
         if je < js: continue
         j=np.arange(js, je+1)
         rows_list.append(np.full(j.size, t, dtype=int))
         cols_list.append(j.astype(int))
         data_list.append(2.0 * a[k - j])
         bvec.append(M - float(conv[k]))
      if not rows_list: break
      rows=np.concatenate(rows_list); cols=np.concatenate(cols_list); data=np.concatenate(data_list)
      brow=len(bvec)
      all_rows=np.concatenate([rows, rows]) # tau column
      # Wait tau column rows should same row indices repeated once; rows_list already row index per constraint. rows length = total nonzeros. all_rows len total*2.
      all_cols=np.concatenate([cols, np.full(brow, n, dtype=int)])
      all_data=np.concatenate([data, np.ones(brow)])
      A=sp.csr_matrix((all_data,(all_rows, all_cols)), shape=(brow,n+1))
      cvec=np.zeros(n+1); cvec[n]=-1.0
      lower=np.concatenate([-a, np.array([0.0])])
      # U
      finite=a[a>1e-12]
      med=float(np.median(finite)) if finite.size else 1.0/n
      U=min(0.1, max(0.01, 10*med, max(float(a.max()),0.0)))
      upper=np.concatenate([np.full(n, U), np.array([0.5*M])])
      Aeq=np.ones(n+1); Aeq[n]=0.0
      try:
         rem=max(1.0, end-time.monotonic())
         res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.asarray(bvec), A_eq=Aeq.reshape(1,-1), b_eq=0.0, bounds=list(zip(lower, upper)), method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': min(5.0, rem*0.8)})
      except Exception:
         res=None
       delta=None
       if res is not None and res.status == 0:
          delta=res.x[:n]
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)): delta=None
       if delta is None or not np.any(np.abs(delta)>1e-14):
          stall +=1
          if K > 16:
             K=max(16, K//2)
          elif stall > 8:
             break
          # try a tiny random feasible step? perturb
          if stall % 4 == 0:
             noise=rng.standard_normal(n); noise-=noise.mean(); a=normalize(a + 0.001*noise); conv=... best? 
          continue
       # line search
       obj_local=fast_score(a)
       best_trial=obj_local; best_alpha=0.0
       alphas=[0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]
       # if tau small, maybe smaller alphas; add 0.005
       for alpha in alphas:
          trial=a+alpha*delta
          if trial.min() < -1e-10: continue
          trial=np.maximum(trial,0.0); ss=trial.sum(); if ss<1e-12: continue
          trial=trial/ss
          obj=fast_score(trial)
          if obj < best_trial - 1e-13:
             best_trial=obj; best_alpha=alpha
       if best_alpha > 0 and best_trial < best_obj - 1e-10:
          a=normalize(a + best_alpha*delta)
          obj=fast_score(a)
          if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
             best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
             if K < 96 and stall==0: K=min(96, int(K*1.2)+1)
             if verbose and it%5==0: print
          else:
             a=best_a.copy(); best_obj=...
       else:
          stall +=1
          if K > 16: K=max(16,K//2)
          elif stall > 8: break
       it+=1
       if it%20==0 and verbose: print
   return best_a,best_obj
```

```
def smooth_lgbfgs(a0, time_limit, T_init=None, maxiter=60, seed=0):
   a=normalize(a0); n=a.size; best...
   if T_init is None: T=max(500.0, 2.0*n)
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=z-z.mean()
   end=...
   no=0; T_best=T
   while time < end-1:
      obj=SmoothObjective(T,n)
      try:
         res=minimize(obj, z, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, maxiter=maxiter, options={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxcor':10,'maxls':50})
      except Exception: res=None
      if res is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
         an=softmax(res.x); sc=fast_score(an)
         if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_obj - 1e-10:
            best_obj=sc; best_a=an.copy(); z=res.x.copy(); z=z-z.mean(); no=0; T_best=T
         else: no+=1
      if no >= 2: break
      T=min(T*1.8, max(5000.0, 10.0*n))
      if T >= max(5000.0,10.0*n) - 1e-9 and no >= 1: break
   return best_a,best_obj
```

#### `optimize_candidate` detailed with no nested
Need use `fast_score` after each method. Use `end` and while.
```
def optimize_candidate(a, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=1):
   a=normalize(a)
   if a.size==0: return a, inf
   best_a=a; best_obj=fast_score(a)
   n=len(a)
   end=time.monotonic()+float(time_limit)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed*99991 + n)
   cycle=0
   no_improve=0
   while time.monotonic() < end - 3.0:
      rem=end-time.monotonic()
      if rem <= 5: break
      # LP
      if n <= 4096:
         t_lp=min(30.0, rem*0.40)
         if t_lp > 4:
            a2,o2=lp_active_descent(best_a, t_lp, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_obj - 1e-10:
                best_a=a2; best_obj=o2; no_improve=0
            else: no_improve +=1
      # Top MD
      t_md=min(30.0, (end-time.monotonic())*0.35)
      if t_md > 4:
         start=best_a if no_improve==0 else best_a
         a2,o2=top_mirror_descent(best_a, t_md, top_k=48, lr_init=0.2, seed=int(seed+cycle*17), verbose=False)
         if ...
      # Dense MD every 2 cycles
      if cycle % 2 == 1:
         t_ds=min(20.0, (end-time.monotonic())*0.2)
         if t_ds > 4 and n<=4096:
            a2,o2=dense_smooth_mirror_descent(best_a, t_ds)
            ...
      # L-BFGS if no_improve >= 2
      if no_improve >= 2 and n <= 2048:
         t_lb=min(25.0, (end-time.monotonic())*0.15)
         if t_lb > 3:
            a2,o2=smooth_lgbfgs(best_a, t_lb)
            ...
      # perturb every 3 cycles if no_improve >=3
      if no_improve >= 3:
         t_p=min(20.0, (end-time.monotonic())*0.15)
         if t_p > 3:
             scale=0.002 + 0.001*min(no_improve-3,5)
             ap=normalize(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n)*scale)
             a2,o2=top_mirror_descent(ap, t_p, top_k=32, seed=int(seed+cycle*31+7))
             if o2<best_obj...
             no_improve=0
      cycle +=1
   # final polish
   rem=end-time.monotonic()
   if rem > 10:
      a2,o2=top_mirror_descent(best_a, max(1.0, rem-5), top_k=64, lr_init=0.1, seed=seed+100)
      if o2<best_obj: ...
      rem=end-time.monotonic()
      if rem > 10 and n <= 1536:
          a2,o2=smooth_lgbfgs(best_a, rem-5)
          ...
   return best_a,best_obj
```

```
def compute_subgradient_top(a, peak_indices, weights):
    n=len(a); g=np.zeros(n)
    for k,w in zip(peak_indices, weights):
       w=float(w)
       if abs(w)<1e-15: continue
       k=int(k); js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)
       if je<js: continue
       idx=np.arange(js,je+1)
       g[idx] += (2.0*w) * a[k-idx]
    return g
```
`weights` maybe numpy array.

### `softmax` robust:
```
def softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
       z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    zm=z-np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(zm)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
       # put all mass on max indices
       e=np.zeros_like(z); e[np.argmax(z)]=1.0; s=1
    return e/s
```
If z has huge positive 1000, max subtract, exp ok. If all -inf? nan_to_num.

### `fast_convolve`: if a not finite, handle? Assume.

### `fast_score`: If a sum invalid return inf.

### `normalize` if a contains nan? Use nan_to_num.
```
def normalize(a):
 a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
 a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
 a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
 s=a.sum()
 if not finite or s<1e-15: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size if a.size else np.array([1.0])
 return a/s
```
If size 0 return empty; but later handle.

### Let's manually inspect possible errors in LP A matrix with `all_rows=np.concatenate([rows, rows])`. Row indices after concatenation length N (total coefficients). For tau column, row indices should be `rows` repeated once, length N, but each constraint row should have coefficient 1 for tau. If `rows` has repeated row indices for each coefficient, adding tau column with same rows repeated N times would add multiple 1s for tau in row t (one per coefficient), resulting coefficient = number of coefficients, wrong! Need tau column coefficient 1 per constraint, not per nonzero. Use a separate row index array `constraint_rows = np.arange(brow)` for tau. So `all_rows = np.concatenate([rows, np.arange(brow)])`; `all_cols = np.concatenate([cols, np.full(brow,n)])`; `all_data = concatenate([data, ones(brow)])`. Great catch.

### Direct `rows` length N, each row repeated for coefficients. Good.

### `A_eq` row length n+1 with ones for δ, 0 τ. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2
by combining:
  * diversified parametric and random smooth initializations,
  * active-set linear-programming descent steps on linearized peak constraints,
  * multiplicative/mirror-descent subgradient polishing,
  * dense log-sum-exp mirror descent,
  * L-BFGS on a smoothed softmax parameterization,
  * rescaling/interpolation to beneficial lengths.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
import scipy.sparse as sp


def normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.array([1.0])
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=float) / a.size
    return a / s


def softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    zm = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(zm)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.zeros_like(z)
        e[int(np.argmax(z))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    return e / s


def fast_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)[:need]


def fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * M)


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = normalize(a)
    old_n = a.size
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return normalize(b)


def compute_subgradient_top(a, peak_indices, weights):
    n = a.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for k, w in zip(peak_indices, weights):
        w = float(w)
        if abs(w) < 1e-16:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        js = max(0, k - n + 1)
        je = min(n - 1, k)
        if je < js:
            continue
        idx = np.arange(js, je + 1)
        g[idx] += (2.0 * w) * a[k - idx]
    return g


def weighted_gradient_fft(a, w):
    """
    Returns g_j = 2 * sum_k w_k a_{k-j} for j=0..n-1,
    for linear convolution c_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}.
    """
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    need = 3 * n - 2
    size = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    h = np.fft.irfft(A * W, size)[:need]
    idx = (2 * n - 2) - np.arange(n)
    return 2.0 * h[idx]


def score_candidates(cands, top=12):
    arr = []
    for a in cands:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
        if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
            continue
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
            continue
        a = a / s
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        sc = float(2.0 * a.size * np.max(c))
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            arr.append((sc, a.copy()))
    if not arr:
        return []
    vals = np.array([x[0] for x in arr], dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(vals, kind="mergesort")
    out = []
    for i in order:
        if len(out) >= top:
            break
        out.append(arr[i][1])
    return out


def generate_initial_candidates(parent, seed=0):
    cands = []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    sizes = []
    if parent is not None:
        p = normalize(parent)
        cands.append(p)
        nset = set()
        base = p.size
        for m in [256, 384, 512, 640, 768, 896, 1024, 1152, 1536, 2048]:
            if m in nset or m > max(4096, base * 2):
                continue
            if 64 <= m <= 4096 and m != base:
                cands.append(interpolate_sequence(p, m))
                nset.add(m)
        for m in [max(256, base // 2), base, min(4096, 2 * base)]:
            m = int(m)
            if m not in sizes and 64 <= m <= 4096:
                sizes.append(m)
    else:
        sizes = [512, 768, 1024]

    sizes = sizes[:4]
    if not sizes:
        sizes = [768]

    for n in sizes:
        i = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
        x = (i + 0.5) / float(max(1, n))

        # Left-power laws / singularities.
        for beta in [0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5]:
            if beta == 0.0:
                cands.append(np.ones(n))
            else:
                cands.append(np.power(i + 1.0, -beta))

        # Left-power times right taper.
        for beta in [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.8]:
            if beta == 0.0:
                base = np.ones(n)
            else:
                base = np.power(i + 1.0, -beta)
            for gamma in [0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0]:
                val = base * np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - i / float(max(1, n - 1)), 1e-8), gamma)
                cands.append(val)

        # Beta-like powers on (0,1).
        for ae in [-0.4, -0.2, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0]:
            for be in [-0.4, -0.2, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0]:
                xx = np.maximum(x, 1e-6)
                yy = np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-6)
                val = np.exp(ae * np.log(xx) + be * np.log(yy))
                cands.append(val)

        # Exponential tilts.
        for lam in np.linspace(-3.0, 3.0, 13):
            cands.append(np.exp(lam * x))

        # Exponential plus quadratic curvature.
        for lam in [1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0]:
            for mu in [-2.0, 2.0, 6.0]:
                cands.append(np.exp(lam * x + mu * (x - 0.5) ** 2))

        # Piecewise candidate: left singular plus gaussian bump.
        for lb in [0.3, 0.5, 0.8]:
            base = np.power(i + 1.0, -lb)
            for cc in [0.3, 0.5, 0.7]:
                for ss in [0.1, 0.2, 0.3]:
                    val = base + 0.5 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - cc) / ss) ** 2)
                    cands.append(val)

    # Random smooth Fourier-like starts.
    for n in sizes[:2]:
        x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(max(1, n))
        for _ in range(30):
            z = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            D = int(rng.integers(2, 10))
            for m in range(1, D + 1):
                freq = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0)) * float(m)
                amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0)) / (float(m) ** 0.7)
                phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
            cands.append(np.exp(z))
            bias_beta = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.7))
            cands.append(np.exp(z) * np.power(np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 1.0, -bias_beta))

    return cands


def top_mirror_descent(a0, time_limit, top_k=32, lr_init=0.2, seed=123, verbose=False):
    a = normalize(a0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    n = a.size
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    a = softmax(z)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = fast_score(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(time_limit)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed * 1000003 + n)

    lr = float(lr_init)
    stale = 0
    temp_factor = 0.05
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end - 0.5:
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        K = min(max(1, int(top_k)), c.size)
        if K < c.size:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(c.size)

        pv = c[idx]
        pvmax = float(np.max(pv))
        pvmin = float(np.min(pv))
        gap = max(pvmax - pvmin, pvmax * 1e-6, 1e-15)
        temp = max(1e-15, temp_factor * gap)

        scaled = (pv - pvmax) / temp
        ex = np.exp(scaled)
        s = float(ex.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0:
            w = ex / s
        else:
            w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / idx.size)

        g = compute_subgradient_top(a, idx, w)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gnorm > 1e-14:
            g = g / gnorm
        else:
            break

        best_trial = M
        best_alpha = 0.0
        for mult in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625]:
            alpha = lr * mult
            if alpha <= 1e-10:
                break
            zt = z - alpha * g
            zt = zt - float(np.max(zt))
            at = softmax(zt)
            obj = fast_score(at)
            if obj < best_trial - 1e-15:
                best_trial = obj
                best_alpha = alpha
                if best_trial < M * 0.999:
                    break

        if best_alpha > 0.0 and best_trial < M - 1e-14:
            z = z - best_alpha * g
            z = z - float(np.max(z))
            a = softmax(z)
            obj = fast_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = obj
                best_a = a.copy()
                stale = 0
                lr = min(lr * 1.15, 1.0)
            else:
                stale += 1
        else:
            stale += 1
            lr = max(lr * 0.5, 1e-5)
            if stale > 300:
                scale = 0.001 + 0.001 * min(float(stale) / 1000.0, 1.0)
                ap = normalize(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * scale)
                a = ap
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                z = z - float(np.max(z))
                lr = max(lr_init * 0.5, 0.05)
                temp_factor = 0.15
                stale = 0

        temp_factor = min(temp_factor * 1.004, 0.5)
        lr = max(lr * 0.99999, 1e-6)
        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print(f"    MD iter {it}: best={best_obj:.9f} lr={lr:.5f} tempf={temp_factor:.3f}")

    return best_a, float(best_obj)


def dense_smooth_mirror_descent(a0, time_limit, T_init=None, lr_init=0.2, seed=0, verbose=False):
    a = normalize(a0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    n = a.size
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    a = softmax(z)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = fast_score(a)
    T = float(T_init) if T_init is not None else max(500.0, 2.0 * float(n))
    T_max = max(5000.0, 10.0 * float(n))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(time_limit)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed * 99991 + n)
    lr = float(lr_init)
    stale = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end - 0.5:
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        scaled = (c - M) * T
        scaled = scaled - float(np.max(scaled))
        ex = np.exp(scaled)
        s = float(ex.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0:
            w = ex / s
        else:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0

        g = weighted_gradient_fft(a, w)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gnorm > 1e-14:
            g = g / gnorm
        else:
            break

        best_trial = M
        best_alpha = 0.0
        for mult in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625]:
            alpha = lr * mult
            if alpha <= 1e-10:
                break
            zt = z - alpha * g
            zt = zt - float(np.max(zt))
            at = softmax(zt)
            obj = fast_score(at)
            if obj < best_trial - 1e-15:
                best_trial = obj
                best_alpha = alpha
                if best_trial < M * 0.999:
                    break

        if best_alpha > 0.0 and best_trial < M - 1e-14:
            z = z - best_alpha * g
            z = z - float(np.max(z))
            a = softmax(z)
            obj = fast_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = obj
                best_a = a.copy()
                stale = 0
                lr = min(lr * 1.1, 1.0)
            else:
                stale += 1
        else:
            stale += 1
            lr = max(lr * 0.5, 1e-5)
            if stale > 400:
                scale = 0.001 + 0.001 * min(float(stale) / 1000.0, 1.0)
                ap = normalize(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * scale)
                a = ap
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                z = z - float(np.max(z))
                lr = max(lr_init * 0.5, 0.05)
                stale = 0

        T = min(T * 1.02, T_max)
        lr = max(lr * 0.99999, 1e-6)
        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print(f"    DMD iter {it}: best={best_obj:.9f} T={T:.0f}")

    return best_a, float(best_obj)


def lp_active_descent(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=0):
    a = normalize(a0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = fast_score(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(time_limit)
    K = min(48, n)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed * 77777 + n)

    while time.monotonic() < end - 1.0:
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        L = c.size
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        Kcur = min(max(8, K), L)
        if Kcur < L:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -Kcur)[-Kcur:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(L)

        rows_list = []
        cols_list = []
        data_list = []
        bvec = []

        for t, k in enumerate(idx):
            k = int(k)
            js = max(0, k - n + 1)
            je = min(n - 1, k)
            if je < js:
                continue
            j = np.arange(js, je + 1, dtype=int)
            rows_list.append(np.full(j.size, t, dtype=int))
            cols_list.append(j)
            data_list.append(2.0 * a[k - j])
            bvec.append(max(0.0, M - float(c[k])))

        if not rows_list or not bvec:
            break

        rows = np.concatenate(rows_list)
        cols = np.concatenate(cols_list)
        data = np.concatenate(data_list)
        brow = len(bvec)

        all_rows = np.concatenate([rows, np.arange(brow, dtype=int)])
        all_cols = np.concatenate([cols, np.full(brow, n, dtype=int)])
        all_data = np.concatenate([data, np.ones(brow, dtype=float)])
        A = sp.csr_matrix((all_data, (all_rows, all_cols)), shape=(brow, n + 1))

        cvec = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
        cvec[n] = -1.0

        pos = a[a > 1e-14]
        med = float(np.median(pos)) if pos.size else 1.0 / n
        U = min(0.1, max(0.01, 10.0 * med, float(a.max())))
        lower = np.concatenate([-a, np.array([0.0])])
        upper = np.concatenate([np.full(n, U, dtype=float), np.array([0.2 * M])])
        Aeq = np.ones(n + 1, dtype=float)
        Aeq[n] = 0.0
        b_eq = np.array([0.0])

        delta = None
        try:
            bounds = list(zip(lower, upper))
            res = linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.asarray(bvec, dtype=float),
                A_eq=Aeq.reshape(1, -1),
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
            if res is not None and res.status == 0 and res.x is not None:
                cand_delta = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(cand_delta)) and np.any(np.abs(cand_delta) > 1e-14):
                    delta = cand_delta
        except Exception:
            delta = None

        if delta is None:
            stall += 1
            if K > 16:
                K = max(16, K // 2)
            elif stall > 8:
                break
            if stall % 5 == 0:
                noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
                noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
                a = normalize(a + 0.001 * noise)
            continue

        obj_local = fast_score(a)
        best_trial = obj_local
        best_alpha = 0.0
        for alpha in [0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0]:
            trial = a + alpha * delta
            if trial.min() < -1e-10:
                continue
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
            s = float(trial.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
                continue
            trial = trial / s
            obj = fast_score(trial)
            if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_trial - 1e-13:
                best_trial = obj
                best_alpha = alpha

        if best_alpha > 0.0 and best_trial < best_obj - 1e-10:
            a = normalize(a + best_alpha * delta)
            obj = fast_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = obj
                best_a = a.copy()
                stall = 0
                if K < 96:
                    K = int(min(96, K * 1.2 + 1))
                if verbose and it % 5 == 0:
                    print(f"    LP iter {it}: best={best_obj:.9f} K={K}")
        else:
            stall += 1
            if K > 16:
                K = max(16, K // 2)
            elif stall > 8:
                break

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 20 == 0:
            print(f"    LP iter {it}: obj={obj_local:.9f} best={best_obj:.9f} stall={stall}")

    return best_a, float(best_obj)


class SmoothObjective:
    def __init__(self, T, n):
        self.T = float(T)
        self.n = int(n)

    def __call__(self, z):
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
        a = softmax(z)
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        scaled = (c - M) * self.T
        scaled = scaled - float(np.max(scaled))
        ex = np.exp(scaled)
        s = float(ex.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            w = ex / s
            F = self.n * 2.0 * (M + np.log(s) / self.T)
        else:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            F = self.n * 2.0 * M

        g = weighted_gradient_fft(a, w)
        A = float(np.dot(a, g))
        grad = self.n * 2.0 * a * (g - A)

        if not np.isfinite(F):
            F = 1e100
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            grad = np.zeros_like(z)
        return float(F), grad


def smooth_lgbfgs(a0, time_limit, T_init=None, maxiter=70, seed=0):
    a = normalize(a0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = fast_score(a)
    T = float(T_init) if T_init is not None else max(500.0, 2.0 * float(n))
    T_top = max(5000.0, 10.0 * float(n))

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(time_limit)
    no_improve = 0
    iterations = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end - 1.0:
        obj_fc = SmoothObjective(T, n)
        try:
            bounds = [(-30.0, 30.0)] * n
            res = minimize(
                obj_fc,
                z,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                maxiter=int(maxiter),
                options={"ftol": 1e-13, "gtol": 1e-10, "maxcor": 10, "maxls": 50},
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
            an = softmax(res.x)
            sc = fast_score(an)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_obj - 1e-10:
                best_obj = sc
                best_a = an.copy()
                z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
                z = z - float(np.mean(z))
                z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        iterations += 1
        if no_improve >= 2:
            break
        T = min(T * 1.7, T_top)
        if T >= T_top - 1e-9 and no_improve >= 1:
            break

    return best_a, float(best_obj)


def optimize_candidate(a, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=1):
    a = normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_obj):
        return a, best_obj

    n = a.size
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(time_limit)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed * 99991 + n)
    cycle = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end - 3.0:
        rem = end - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 5.0:
            break

        # LP active-set descent.
        if n <= 2048:
            t_lp = min(30.0, rem * 0.38)
            if t_lp > 4.0:
                a2, o2 = lp_active_descent(best_a, t_lp, verbose=verbose, seed=int(seed + cycle * 7))
                if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_obj - 1e-10:
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    best_obj = float(o2)
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

        # Top-peak mirror descent.
        t_md = min(30.0, (end - time.monotonic()) * 0.34)
        if t_md > 4.0:
            a2, o2 = top_mirror_descent(best_a, t_md, top_k=48, lr_init=0.2,
                                        seed=int(seed + cycle * 13 + 1), verbose=verbose)
            if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_obj - 1e-10:
                best_a = a2.copy()
                best_obj = float(o2)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        # Dense smooth mirror descent periodically.
        if cycle % 2 == 1:
            t_ds = min(20.0, (end - time.monotonic()) * 0.18)
            if t_ds > 4.0 and n <= 4096:
                a2, o2 = dense_smooth_mirror_descent(best_a, t_ds, T_init=max(800.0, 2.0 * n),
                                                     lr_init=0.15, seed=int(seed + cycle * 29 + 2),
                                                     verbose=verbose)
                if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_obj - 1e-10:
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    best_obj = float(o2)
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

        # L-BFGS on smoothed softmax param.
        if no_improve >= 2 and n <= 1536:
            t_lb = min(25.0, (end - time.monotonic()) * 0.16)
            if t_lb > 4.0:
                a2, o2 = smooth_lgbfgs(best_a, t_lb, T_init=max(700.0, 2.0 * n),
                                       maxiter=50, seed=int(seed + cycle * 41 + 3))
                if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_obj - 1e-10:
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    best_obj = float(o2)
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

        # Perturb and re-polish if stuck.
        if no_improve >= 3:
            t_p = min(20.0, (end - time.monotonic()) * 0.14)
            if t_p > 3.0:
                scale = 0.002 + 0.001 * min(float(no_improve - 3), 5.0)
                ap = normalize(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * scale)
                a2, o2 = top_mirror_descent(ap, t_p, top_k=32, lr_init=0.18,
                                            seed=int(seed + cycle * 53 + 5))
                if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_obj - 1e-10:
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    best_obj = float(o2)
                no_improve = 0

        cycle += 1

    # Final polish.
    rem = end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 10.0:
        a2, o2 = top_mirror_descent(best_a, max(1.0, rem - 5.0), top_k=64, lr_init=0.1,
                                    seed=int(seed + 100))
        if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_obj - 1e-10:
            best_a = a2.copy()
            best_obj = float(o2)

        rem = end - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 10.0 and n <= 1536:
            a2, o2 = smooth_lgbfgs(best_a, rem - 5.0, T_init=max(2000.0, 5.0 * n),
                                   maxiter=40, seed=int(seed + 200))
            if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_obj - 1e-10:
                best_a = a2.copy()
                best_obj = float(o2)

    return best_a, float(best_obj)


def resize_candidates(a, max_n=4096):
    a = normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    cands = []
    seen = set()
    possible = []
    if n > 256:
        possible.append(max(128, n // 2))
    for m in [int(n * 1.3), int(n * 1.5), n * 2, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048, 3072, 4096]:
        possible.append(int(m))
    for m in possible:
        if 64 <= m <= max_n and m not in seen:
            seen.add(m)
            cands.append(interpolate_sequence(a, m))
    return score_candidates(cands, top=4)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0)

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_seq = None

    # Parent sequence from previous constructions.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and p.sum() > 1e-12:
            parent = p / p.sum()
            ps = fast_score(parent)
            if np.isfinite(ps) and ps < best_score:
                best_score = float(ps)
                best_seq = parent.copy()
            try:
                ev = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in parent]))
                if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_score:
                    best_score = ev
                    best_seq = parent.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass
            print(f"Parent: n={parent.size}, fast_score={ps:.8f}, best={best_score:.8f}")
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Generate and score diverse starts.
    print("Generating initial candidates...")
    gen = generate_initial_candidates(parent, seed=int(seed))
    starts_all = score_candidates(gen, top=8)

    # Ensure the current best sequence is first if it exists.
    starts = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        starts.append(best_seq)
    for a in starts_all:
        if len(starts) >= 5:
            break
        starts.append(a)

    if not starts:
        starts = [np.ones(768, dtype=float) / 768.0]

    # Main optimization over several starts.
    n_starts = min(len(starts), 5)
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    alloc = max(60.0, rem * 0.50)
    time_per = max(25.0, alloc / float(max(1, n_starts)))

    for idx, st in enumerate(starts[:n_starts]):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < deadline - 120.0:
            t = min(time_per, deadline - now - 120.0)
            if t < 10.0:
                break
            print(f"Optimizing start {idx}: n={st.size}, initial fast={fast_score(st):.8f}, t={t:.1f}s")
            a_opt, o_opt = optimize_candidate(st, t, verbose=False, seed=int(seed + idx * 101 + 17))
            if np.isfinite(o_opt) and o_opt < best_score:
                best_score = float(o_opt)
                best_seq = a_opt.copy()
                print(f"  NEW BEST: {best_score:.8f} (n={best_seq.size})")

    # Try resizing the best sequence to better lengths.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_seq is not None and rem > 120.0:
        print("Trying better lengths...")
        resized = resize_candidates(best_seq, max_n=4096)
        resized_best = None
        resized_obj = float("inf")
        for r in resized:
            val = fast_score(r)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
                best_score = float(val)
                best_seq = r.copy()
                print(f"  RESIZE IMPROVED: {best_score:.8f} (n={best_seq.size})")
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < resized_obj:
                resized_obj = val
                resized_best = r

        if resized_best is not None:
            t = max(30.0, rem - 120.0) * 0.45
            if t > 20.0:
                a_opt, o_opt = optimize_candidate(resized_best, t, verbose=False, seed=int(seed + 999))
                if np.isfinite(o_opt) and o_opt < best_score:
                    best_score = float(o_opt)
                    best_seq = a_opt.copy()
                    print(f"  RESIZE OPTIMIZED NEW BEST: {best_score:.8f} (n={best_seq.size})")

    # Final small polish if time remains.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_seq is not None and rem > 30.0:
        t = rem - 10.0
        if t > 10.0:
            a_opt, o_opt = optimize_candidate(best_seq, t, verbose=False, seed=int(seed + 4321))
            if np.isfinite(o_opt) and o_opt < best_score:
                best_score = float(o_opt)
                best_seq = a_opt.copy()

    # Fallback.
    if best_seq is None or not np.isfinite(fast_score(best_seq)):
        best_seq = np.ones(768, dtype=float) / 768.0

    # Add a tiny floor only if necessary.
    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=float)
    best_seq = np.nan_to_num(best_seq, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        best_seq = np.ones(768, dtype=float) / 768.0
    else:
        best_seq = best_seq / s

    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_seq]

    # Final verification using the official evaluator.
    try:
        fs = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
    except Exception:
        fs = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(fs):
        # Very conservative fallback.
        fallback = np.ones(768, dtype=float) / 768.0
        final_list = [float(x) for x in fallback]
        try:
            fs = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
        except Exception:
            fs = float("inf")

    print(f"FINAL SCORE: {fs:.8f} (n={len(final_list)})")
    return final_list
```
